```python
"""Remote-only evidence collector authored locally by the research lead.

Execute in Muse's existing tool environment only. No interpretation, merchant
edits, extra requests, source/binary export, or hidden trace extraction.
"""
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import time
import zipfile

def now(): return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
def digest(path): return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
conditions = {
    'C0': ['--query', 'stainless steel frying pan'],
    'C1': ['--query', 'Misen stainless steel frying pan'],
    'C2': ['--query', 'stainless steel frying pan', '--brand', 'Misen'],
    'C3': ['--query', 'stainless steel frying pan', '--prefer-brand', 'Misen'],
    'C4': ['--query', 'stainless steel frying pan', '--domain', 'misen.com'],
    'C5': ['--query', 'stainless steel frying pan', '--brand', 'Misen', '--domain', 'misen.com'],
    'C6': ['--query', 'stainless steel frying pan', '--prefer-brand', 'Misen', '--domain', 'misen.com'],
}
common = ['-n', '50', '--conversation-id', 'misen-input-experiment-2026-09-26',
          '--retries', '0', '--timeout-secs', '30', '--raw', '--no-save']
orders = [['C0','C1','C2','C3','C4','C5','C6'],
          ['C1','C2','C3','C4','C5','C6','C0'],
          ['C2','C3','C4','C5','C6','C0','C1']]
stamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ')
out=Path.home()/'workspace'/'research-evidence'/('misen-input-'+stamp)
out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
(out/'collector.py').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
(out/'schedule.json').write_text(json.dumps({'conditions':conditions,'common_args':common,'orders':orders},indent=2)+'\n')
executions=[]
unavailable=False
for block, order in enumerate(orders,1):
    for slot,condition in enumerate(order,1):
        number=(block-1)*7+slot
        run=f'R{number:02}'
        stem=f'{run}-{condition}'
        argv=['/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search']+conditions[condition]+common
        (out/(stem+'.argv.json')).write_text(json.dumps(argv,indent=2)+'\n')
        stdout=out/(stem+'.stdout.raw-bytes');stderr=out/(stem+'.stderr.raw-bytes')
        start=now();clock=time.monotonic();exit_code=None;error=None
        with stdout.open('wb') as so, stderr.open('wb') as se:
            try:
                process=subprocess.run(argv,stdout=so,stderr=se,timeout=45,check=False)
                exit_code=process.returncode
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                error='Collector timeout after 45 seconds; command not retried.'
            except (FileNotFoundError,PermissionError) as exc:
                error=f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'
                unavailable=True
        record={'run_id':run,'block':block,'slot':slot,'condition_id':condition,
                'argv_exact_array':argv,'utc_start':start,'utc_end':now(),
                'elapsed_ms':round((time.monotonic()-clock)*1000,3),
                'exit_code':exit_code,'observed_error':error,
                'stdout_bytes':stdout.stat().st_size,'stdout_sha256':digest(stdout),'stdout_file':stdout.name,
                'stderr_bytes':stderr.stat().st_size,'stderr_sha256':digest(stderr),'stderr_file':stderr.name,
                'conversation_id':'misen-input-experiment-2026-09-26',
                'query':conditions[condition][1],'num_results':50,'retry_count':0,
                'origin':'written by collector from this subprocess invocation; not manually transcribed'}
        (out/(stem+'.execution.json')).write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')
        executions.append(record)
        if unavailable:break
    if unavailable:break
(out/'collection-status.json').write_text(json.dumps({'scheduled':21,'attempted':len(executions),'stopped_for_launch_error':unavailable,'completed_utc':now()},indent=2)+'\n')
files=sorted(p for p in out.iterdir() if p.is_file())
with (out/'MANIFEST.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.writer(f);w.writerow(['path','bytes','sha256'])
    for p in files:w.writerow([p.name,p.stat().st_size,digest(p)])
archive=out.with_suffix('.zip')
with zipfile.ZipFile(archive,'x',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
    for p in sorted(out.iterdir()):
        if p.is_file():z.write(p,arcname=out.name+'/'+p.name)
with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as z:assert z.testzip() is None
print(json.dumps({'archive_path':str(archive),'bytes':archive.stat().st_size,'sha256':digest(archive),'scheduled':21,'attempted':len(executions),'exit_codes':[r['exit_code'] for r in executions]},indent=2))

```python
"""Research-lead-authored remote collector. Run once in Muse; no analysis."""
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv, hashlib, json, random, subprocess, time, zipfile
def now(): return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
def sha(p): return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
cases=[('A','All-Clad','all-clad.com','stainless steel frying pan'),('L','Levoit','levoit.com','small air purifier for a bedroom'),('H','Hydro Flask','hydroflask.com','32 oz stainless steel insulated water bottle')]
conditions=['C0','C1','C2','C3','C4','C5','C6']
rng=random.Random(20260927);schedule=[]
for block in range(1,4):
    cells=[(c,k) for c in cases for k in conditions];rng.shuffle(cells)
    for slot,((case,brand,domain,query),condition) in enumerate(cells,1):
        flags={'C0':[],'C1':[],'C2':['--brand',brand],'C3':['--prefer-brand',brand],'C4':['--domain',domain],'C5':['--brand',brand,'--domain',domain],'C6':['--prefer-brand',brand,'--domain',domain]}[condition]
        q=brand+' '+query if condition=='C1' else query
        argv=['/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search','--query',q]+flags+['-n','50','--conversation-id','brand-replication-20260927-'+case,'--retries','0','--timeout-secs','30','--raw','--no-save']
        n=len(schedule)+1
        schedule.append(dict(run_id=f'R{n:02}',stem=f'R{n:02}-{case}-{condition}',block=block,slot=slot,case=case,condition=condition,brand=brand,domain=domain,argv=argv))
stamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ')

    stem=item['stem'];argv=item['argv']
    (out/(stem+'.argv.json')).write_text(json.dumps(argv,indent=2)+'\n')

    start=now();clock=time.monotonic();code=None;error=None
    with stdout.open('wb') as so,stderr.open('wb') as se:
        try: code=subprocess.run(argv,stdout=so,stderr=se,timeout=45,check=False).returncode
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired: error='Collector timeout after45 seconds; no retry.'
        except (FileNotFoundError,PermissionError) as exc:
            error=f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}';unavailable=True
    record=dict(item,utc_start=start,utc_end=now(),elapsed_ms=round((time.monotonic()-clock)*1000,3),exit_code=code,observed_error=error,stdout_file=stdout.name,stdout_bytes=stdout.stat().st_size,stdout_sha256=sha(stdout),stderr_file=stderr.name,stderr_bytes=stderr.stat().st_size,stderr_sha256=sha(stderr),origin='Collector recorded this subprocess invocation; no manual transcription.')
    (out/(stem+'.execution.json')).write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')
    executions.append(record)
    if unavailable: break
(out/'collection-status.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(scheduled=len(schedule),attempted=len(executions),stopped_for_launch_error=unavailable,completed_utc=now()),indent=2)+'\n')
files=sorted(out.iterdir())
with (out/'MANIFEST.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.writer(f);w.writerow(['path','bytes','sha256'])
    for p in files: w.writerow([p.name,p.stat().st_size,sha(p)])
archive=out.with_suffix('.zip')
with zipfile.ZipFile(archive,'x',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
    for p in sorted(out.iterdir()): z.write(p,arcname=out.name+'/'+p.name)
with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as z: assert z.testzip() is None
print(json.dumps(dict(archive_path=str(archive),bytes=archive.stat().st_size,sha256=sha(archive),scheduled=len(schedule),attempted=len(executions),exit_codes=[r['exit_code'] for r in executions]),indent=2))

"""Research-lead-authored follow-up collection, run remotely once. No analysis."""
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime,timezone
import csv,hashlib,json,random,subprocess,time,zipfile

schedule=[];rng=random.Random(20260928)
def add(stage,case,condition,block,argv,domain='',brand='',output=False):
    n=len(schedule)+1
    schedule.append(dict(run_id=f'R{n:02}',stem=f'R{n:02}-{stage}-{case}-{condition}',stage=stage,case=case,condition=condition,block=block,domain=domain,brand=brand,argv=argv,parsed_output_requested=output))
def base(q,cid,n=50,brand=None):
    return ['/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search','--query',q]+(['--brand',brand] if brand else [])+['-n',str(n),'--conversation-id',cid,'--retries','0','--timeout-secs','30']
for block in range(1,4):
    cells=[(c,n) for c in cases for n in [10,50,100]];rng.shuffle(cells)
    for (case,brand,domain,q),n in cells:
        add('count',case,'n'+str(n),block,base(q,'count-followup-20260927-'+case,n,brand)+['--raw','--no-save'],domain,brand)
for block in range(1,4):
    cells=[(c,mode) for c in [cases[0],cases[3]] for mode in ['fixed','fresh']];rng.shuffle(cells)
    for (case,brand,domain,q),mode in cells:
        cid='context-followup-20260927-'+case+('-fixed' if mode=='fixed' else '-fresh-'+str(block))
        add('context',case,mode,block,base(q,cid,50,brand)+['--raw','--no-save'],domain,brand)
for case,brand,domain,q in [cases[1],cases[2]]:

        add('bracket',case,str(slot)+'-'+mode,1,base(q,'mode-bracket-20260927-'+case,50,brand)+['--'+mode,'--no-save'],domain,brand)
for mode in ['raw','json','stdout']:
    add('dual','M',mode,1,base(cases[0][3],'mode-dual-20260927-M',50,'Misen')+['--'+mode,'--out','{PARSED_OUT}'],'misen.com','Misen',True)

add('meta','python','version',1,['python3','--version'])

            error=f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}';unavailable=True
    rec=dict(item,argv_actual=argv,utc_start=start,utc_end=now(),elapsed_ms=round((time.monotonic()-clock)*1000,3),exit_code=code,observed_error=error,stdout_file=so.name,stdout_bytes=so.stat().st_size,stdout_sha256=sha(so),stderr_file=se.name,stderr_bytes=se.stat().st_size,stderr_sha256=sha(se),parsed_output_exists=parsed.is_file())
    if parsed.is_file(): rec.update(parsed_output_file=parsed.name,parsed_output_bytes=parsed.stat().st_size,parsed_output_sha256=sha(parsed))

```python
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import random
import subprocess
import zipfile


def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def digest(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ")
    root = Path.cwd() / ("catalog-upstream-fusion-" + stamp)
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    (root / "collector.py").write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    a = "stainless steel frying pan"
    b = "nonstick frying pan"
    conditions = {"A": [a], "B": [b], "AB": [a, b], "BA": [b, a], "AA": [a, a]}
    rng = random.Random(20260927)
    plan = []
    for repeat in range(1, 4):
        names = list(conditions)
        rng.shuffle(names)
        for name in names:
            argv = ["/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search"]
            for query in conditions[name]:
                argv += ["--query", query]
            argv += ["-n", "50", "--conversation-id", "upstream-fusion-20260927-Astra-01", "--retries", "0", "--timeout-secs", "30", "--raw"]
            plan.append({"run_id": f"R{len(plan)+1:02d}-{name}-{repeat}", "condition": name, "repeat": repeat, "argv": argv, "user_id_present": False})
    (root / "plan.json").write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=2) + "\n")
    for item in plan:
        folder = root / item["run_id"]
        folder.mkdir()
        (folder / "argv.json").write_text(json.dumps(item["argv"], indent=2) + "\n")
        start = utc()
        exception = None
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(item["argv"], capture_output=True, timeout=40, check=False)
            stdout, stderr, exit_code = result.stdout, result.stderr, result.returncode
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as error:
            stdout, stderr, exit_code = error.stdout or b"", error.stderr or b"", None
            exception = "collector subprocess timeout at 40 seconds"
        except OSError as error:
            stdout, stderr, exit_code = b"", b"", None
            exception = repr(error)
        end = utc()
        (folder / "stdout.bin").write_bytes(stdout)
        (folder / "stderr.bin").write_bytes(stderr)
        receipt = dict(item, start_utc=start, end_utc=end, exit_code=exit_code, exception=exception, stdout_bytes=len(stdout), stderr_bytes=len(stderr), stdout_sha256=digest(folder / "stdout.bin"), stderr_sha256=digest(folder / "stderr.bin"))
        (folder / "execution.json").write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2) + "\n")
    files = sorted(path for path in root.rglob("*") if path.is_file())
    with (root / "MANIFEST.csv").open("w", newline="") as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=["path", "bytes", "sha256"])
        writer.writeheader()
        for path in files:
            writer.writerow({"path": path.relative_to(root).as_posix(), "bytes": path.stat().st_size, "sha256": digest(path)})
    archive = root.with_suffix(".zip")
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as bundle:
        for path in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
            if path.is_file():
                bundle.write(path, path.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({"archive_path": str(archive), "bytes": archive.stat().st_size, "sha256": digest(archive), "scheduled_calls": len(plan), "attempted_calls": len(list(root.glob("R*/execution.json")))}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

```python
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import random
import subprocess
import zipfile


def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def digest(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ")
    root = Path.cwd() / ("catalog-upstream-color-" + stamp)
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    (root / "collector.py").write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    query = "32 oz stainless steel insulated water bottle"
    conditions = {"BASE": [], "GREEN": ["--color", "green"], "PREFER": ["--prefer-brand", "Hydro Flask"], "BRAND": ["--brand", "Hydro Flask"]}
    rng = random.Random(20260928)
    plan = []
    for repeat in range(1, 4):
        names = list(conditions)
        rng.shuffle(names)
        for name in names:
            argv = ["/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search", "--query", query] + conditions[name]
            argv += ["-n", "50", "--conversation-id", "upstream-color-20260927-Astra-01", "--retries", "0", "--timeout-secs", "30", "--raw"]
            plan.append({"run_id": f"R{len(plan)+1:02d}-{name}-{repeat}", "condition": name, "repeat": repeat, "argv": argv, "user_id_present": False})
    (root / "plan.json").write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=2) + "\n")
    for item in plan:
        folder = root / item["run_id"]
        folder.mkdir()
        (folder / "argv.json").write_text(json.dumps(item["argv"], indent=2) + "\n")
        start = utc()
        exception = None
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(item["argv"], capture_output=True, timeout=40, check=False)
            stdout, stderr, exit_code = result.stdout, result.stderr, result.returncode
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as error:
            stdout, stderr, exit_code = error.stdout or b"", error.stderr or b"", None
            exception = "collector subprocess timeout at 40 seconds"
        except OSError as error:
            stdout, stderr, exit_code = b"", b"", None
            exception = repr(error)
        end = utc()
        (folder / "stdout.bin").write_bytes(stdout)
        (folder / "stderr.bin").write_bytes(stderr)
        receipt = dict(item, start_utc=start, end_utc=end, exit_code=exit_code, exception=exception, stdout_bytes=len(stdout), stderr_bytes=len(stderr), stdout_sha256=digest(folder / "stdout.bin"), stderr_sha256=digest(folder / "stderr.bin"))
        (folder / "execution.json").write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2) + "\n")
    files = sorted(path for path in root.rglob("*") if path.is_file())
    with (root / "MANIFEST.csv").open("w", newline="") as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=["path", "bytes", "sha256"])
        writer.writeheader()
        for path in files:
            writer.writerow({"path": path.relative_to(root).as_posix(), "bytes": path.stat().st_size, "sha256": digest(path)})
    archive = root.with_suffix(".zip")
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as bundle:
        for path in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
            if path.is_file():
                bundle.write(path, path.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({"archive_path": str(archive), "bytes": archive.stat().st_size, "sha256": digest(archive), "scheduled_calls": len(plan), "attempted_calls": len(list(root.glob("R*/execution.json")))}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```python
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import random
import subprocess
import zipfile


def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def digest(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ")
    root = Path.cwd() / ("catalog-upstream-synonyms-" + stamp)
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    (root / "collector.py").write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    a = "stainless steel frying pan"
    b = "stainless steel skillet"
    conditions = {"A": [a], "B": [b], "AB": [a, b], "BA": [b, a], "AAB": [a, a, b], "ABB": [a, b, b], "JOIN": [a + " " + b]}
    rng = random.Random(20260929)
    plan = []
    for repeat in range(1, 4):
        names = list(conditions)
        rng.shuffle(names)
        for name in names:
            argv = ["/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search"]
            for query in conditions[name]:
                argv += ["--query", query]
            argv += ["-n", "50", "--conversation-id", "upstream-synonyms-20260927-Astra-01", "--retries", "0", "--timeout-secs", "30", "--raw"]
            plan.append({"run_id": f"R{len(plan)+1:02d}-{name}-{repeat}", "condition": name, "repeat": repeat, "argv": argv, "user_id_present": False})
    (root / "plan.json").write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=2) + "\n")
    for item in plan:
        folder = root / item["run_id"]
        folder.mkdir()
        (folder / "argv.json").write_text(json.dumps(item["argv"], indent=2) + "\n")
        start = utc()
        exception = None
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(item["argv"], capture_output=True, timeout=40, check=False)
            stdout, stderr, exit_code = result.stdout, result.stderr, result.returncode
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as error:
            stdout, stderr, exit_code = error.stdout or b"", error.stderr or b"", None
            exception = "collector subprocess timeout at 40 seconds"
        except OSError as error:
            stdout, stderr, exit_code = b"", b"", None
            exception = repr(error)
        end = utc()
        (folder / "stdout.bin").write_bytes(stdout)
        (folder / "stderr.bin").write_bytes(stderr)
        receipt = dict(item, start_utc=start, end_utc=end, exit_code=exit_code, exception=exception, stdout_bytes=len(stdout), stderr_bytes=len(stderr), stdout_sha256=digest(folder / "stdout.bin"), stderr_sha256=digest(folder / "stderr.bin"))
        (folder / "execution.json").write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2) + "\n")
    files = sorted(path for path in root.rglob("*") if path.is_file())
    with (root / "MANIFEST.csv").open("w", newline="") as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=["path", "bytes", "sha256"])
        writer.writeheader()
        for path in files:
            writer.writerow({"path": path.relative_to(root).as_posix(), "bytes": path.stat().st_size, "sha256": digest(path)})
    archive = root.with_suffix(".zip")
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as bundle:
        for path in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
            if path.is_file():
                bundle.write(path, path.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({"archive_path": str(archive), "bytes": archive.stat().st_size, "sha256": digest(archive), "scheduled_calls": len(plan), "attempted_calls": len(list(root.glob("R*/execution.json")))}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

```python
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import zipfile


def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    root = Path.cwd() / ('catalog-receive-trace-' + datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ'))
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    (root / 'collector.py').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    catalog = ['/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search', '--query', 'stainless steel frying pan', '-n', '50', '--conversation-id', 'upstream-receive-20260927-Astra-01', '--retries', '0', '--timeout-secs', '30', '--raw']
    argv = ['/usr/bin/strace', '-f', '-qq', '-ttt', '-xx', '-s', '1048576', '-e', 'trace=connect,recvfrom,recvmsg,recvmmsg,read,readv,write,writev,close', '-e', 'raw=read,readv,write,writev', '-o', str(root / 'receive.strace.txt'), '--'] + catalog
    plan = {'author': 'Astra', 'model': 'gpt-6-astra', 'question': 'Can incoming socket response bytes be compared with raw stdout within this one ordinary invocation?', 'scheduled_calls': 1, 'retries': 0, 'user_id_present': False, 'argv': argv, 'trace_scope': 'This launched command and its descendants only. No send payloads, environment, file-read payloads, memory dumps, attachments to unrelated processes, security changes or replacement transport.', 'stopping_rule': 'Stop after this one attempt. TLS or missing receive bytes leave the body identity unobserved. No automatic retries or instrumentation changes.'}
    (root / 'plan.json').write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=2) + '\n')
    start = utc()
    exception = None
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, timeout=50, check=False)
        stdout, stderr, code = result.stdout, result.stderr, result.returncode
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as error:
        stdout, stderr, code = error.stdout or b'', error.stderr or b'', None
        exception = 'collector timeout after 50 seconds'
    except OSError as error:
        stdout, stderr, code = b'', b'', None
        exception = repr(error)
    end = utc()
    (root / 'stdout.bin').write_bytes(stdout)
    (root / 'stderr.bin').write_bytes(stderr)
    receipt = {'argv': argv, 'start_utc': start, 'end_utc': end, 'exit_code': code, 'exception': exception, 'stdout_sha256': sha(root / 'stdout.bin'), 'stderr_sha256': sha(root / 'stderr.bin'), 'trace_present': (root / 'receive.strace.txt').exists()}
    (root / 'execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2) + '\n')
    files = sorted(p for p in root.rglob('*') if p.is_file())
    with (root / 'MANIFEST.csv').open('w', newline='') as out:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(out, fieldnames=['path', 'bytes', 'sha256'])
        writer.writeheader()
        for p in files:
            writer.writerow({'path': p.relative_to(root).as_posix(), 'bytes': p.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(p)})
    archive = root.with_suffix('.zip')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as out:
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file():
                out.write(p, p.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({'archive_path': str(archive), 'bytes': archive.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(archive), 'attempts': 1, 'exit_code': code, 'exception': exception}))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```python
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import zipfile


def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    root = Path.cwd() / ('catalog-descriptor-trace-' + datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ'))
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    (root / 'collector.py').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    catalog = ['/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search', '--query', 'stainless steel frying pan', '-n', '50', '--conversation-id', 'upstream-descriptor-20260927-Astra-01', '--retries', '0', '--timeout-secs', '30', '--raw']
    argv = ['/usr/bin/strace', '-f', '-qq', '-ttt', '-yy', '-s', '0', '-e', 'trace=connect,sendmsg,recvfrom,recvmsg,recvmmsg,read,readv,write,writev,close,openat,dup,dup2,dup3,fcntl', '-o', str(root / 'descriptor.strace.txt'), '--'] + catalog
    plan = {'author': 'Astra', 'model': 'gpt-6-astra', 'question': 'Which file-descriptor channel carries output for the normal worker-delegating client invocation?', 'scheduled_calls': 1, 'retries': 0, 'user_id_present': False, 'argv': argv, 'trace_scope': 'This launched command and its descendants only. String payloads suppressed with -s 0; descriptor metadata retained. No environment, file or network payload dumps, memory dumps, attachments to unrelated processes, security changes or replacement transport.', 'stopping_rule': 'Stop after one metadata-only attempt. Descriptor relationships do not establish HTTP body identity or the ranking formula. Do not trace the separate worker or probe its socket. No retries or instrumentation changes.'}
    (root / 'plan.json').write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=2) + '\n')
    start = utc()
    exception = None
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, timeout=50, check=False)
        stdout, stderr, code = result.stdout, result.stderr, result.returncode
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as error:
        stdout, stderr, code = error.stdout or b'', error.stderr or b'', None
        exception = 'collector timeout after 50 seconds'
    except OSError as error:
        stdout, stderr, code = b'', b'', None
        exception = repr(error)
    end = utc()
    (root / 'stdout.bin').write_bytes(stdout)
    (root / 'stderr.bin').write_bytes(stderr)
    receipt = {'argv': argv, 'start_utc': start, 'end_utc': end, 'exit_code': code, 'exception': exception, 'stdout_sha256': sha(root / 'stdout.bin'), 'stderr_sha256': sha(root / 'stderr.bin'), 'trace_present': (root / 'descriptor.strace.txt').exists()}
    (root / 'execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2) + '\n')
    files = sorted(p for p in root.rglob('*') if p.is_file())
    with (root / 'MANIFEST.csv').open('w', newline='') as out:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(out, fieldnames=['path', 'bytes', 'sha256'])
        writer.writeheader()
        for p in files:
            writer.writerow({'path': p.relative_to(root).as_posix(), 'bytes': p.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(p)})
    archive = root.with_suffix('.zip')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as out:
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file():
                out.write(p, p.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({'archive_path': str(archive), 'bytes': archive.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(archive), 'attempts': 1, 'exit_code': code, 'exception': exception}))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

Collector:
```python
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv, hashlib, json, subprocess, time, zipfile, sys

def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def main():
    schedule_path=Path(sys.argv[1])
    first,last=int(sys.argv[2]),int(sys.argv[3])
    schedule=json.loads(schedule_path.read_text())
    chosen=schedule[first-1:last]
    assert 1 <= first <= last <= 100 and len(chosen)==last-first+1
    root=Path.cwd()/('merchant-benchmark-'+f'{first:03d}-{last:03d}-'+datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ'))
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    (root/'collector.py').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    (root/'queries.json').write_bytes(schedule_path.read_bytes())
    (root/'batch-plan.json').write_text(json.dumps({'author':'Astra','model':'gpt-6-astra','start_utc':utc(),'first':first,'last':last,'scheduled_queries':chosen,'requested_n':50,'user_id_present':False,'retries':0,'surface':'CLI raw stdout, not authenticated server body','stop':'3 consecutive failures or explicit rate/access/authentication error'},indent=2)+'\n')
    tool=Path('/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search')
    identity={'path':str(tool)}
    try: identity.update({'bytes':tool.stat().st_size,'before_sha256':sha(tool)})
    except OSError as e: identity['before_error']=repr(e)
    errors=0
    for q in chosen:
        p=root/q['id']
        p.mkdir()
        argv=[str(tool),'--query',q['query'],'-n','50','--conversation-id','merchant-benchmark-20260927-Astra-'+q['id'],'--retries','0','--timeout-secs','30','--raw','--no-save']
        receipt={'query':q,'argv':argv,'start_utc':utc(),'exception':None}
        try:
            result=subprocess.run(argv,capture_output=True,timeout=45,check=False)
            stdout,stderr,code=result.stdout,result.stderr,result.returncode
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
            stdout,stderr,code=e.stdout or b'',e.stderr or b'',None
            receipt['exception']='collector timeout after45seconds'
        except OSError as e:
            stdout,stderr,code=b'',b'',None
            receipt['exception']=repr(e)
        (p/'stdout.bin').write_bytes(stdout)
        (p/'stderr.bin').write_bytes(stderr)
        receipt.update({'end_utc':utc(),'exit_code':code,'stdout_bytes':len(stdout),'stderr_bytes':len(stderr),'stdout_sha256':sha(p/'stdout.bin'),'stderr_sha256':sha(p/'stderr.bin')})
        (p/'execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt,indent=2)+'\n')
        success=code==0
        try:
            parsed=json.loads(stdout)
            responses=parsed.get('tool_responses',[])
            if responses and any(x.get('success') is False for x in responses):success=False
        except (ValueError,AttributeError,TypeError):
            success=False
        errors=0 if success else errors+1
        error_text=(stderr+stdout[:4096] if not success else stderr).decode('utf-8','replace').lower()
        denied=any(t in error_text for t in ['rate limit','too many requests','permission denied','unauthorized','authentication failed','access denied'])
        if errors>=3 or denied:
            (root/'STOP.json').write_text(json.dumps({'at_query':q['id'],'consecutive_failures':errors,'access_or_rate_error':denied,'time_utc':utc()},indent=2)+'\n')
            break
        time.sleep(1)
    try: identity['after_sha256']=sha(tool)
    except OSError as e: identity['after_error']=repr(e)
    (root/'tool-identity.json').write_text(json.dumps(identity,indent=2)+'\n')
    files=sorted(p for p in root.rglob('*') if p.is_file())
    with (root/'MANIFEST.csv').open('w',newline='') as out:
        w=csv.DictWriter(out,fieldnames=['path','bytes','sha256'])
        w.writeheader()
        for p in files:w.writerow({'path':p.relative_to(root).as_posix(),'bytes':p.stat().st_size,'sha256':sha(p)})
    archive=root.with_suffix('.zip')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive,'x',zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as out:
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file():out.write(p,p.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({'archive_path':str(archive),'bytes':archive.stat().st_size,'sha256':sha(archive),'attempted':len(list(root.glob('Q*/execution.json'))),'planned':len(chosen),'stopped':(root/'STOP.json').exists()}))

if __name__=='__main__':
    main()
```

```python
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import zipfile


def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    schedule_path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    schedule = json.loads(schedule_path.read_text())
    assert len(schedule) == 60 and len({q['run_id'] for q in schedule}) == 60
    root = Path.cwd() / ('taste-style-' + datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ'))
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    (root / 'collector.py').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    (root / 'schedule.json').write_bytes(schedule_path.read_bytes())
    tool = Path('/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search')
    identity = {'path': str(tool)}
    try:
        identity.update({'before_bytes': tool.stat().st_size, 'before_sha256': sha(tool)})
    except OSError as e:
        identity['before_error'] = repr(e)
    errors = 0
    for q in schedule:
        dest = root / q['run_id']
        dest.mkdir()
        argv = [str(tool), '--query', q['query'], '-n', '50', '--conversation-id', 'taste-style-20260927-Astra-fixed', '--retries', '0', '--timeout-secs', '30', '--raw', '--no-save']
        receipt = {'author': 'Astra (gpt-6-astra)', 'query': q, 'argv': argv, 'start_utc': utc(), 'exception': None}
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, timeout=45, check=False)
            stdout, stderr, code = result.stdout, result.stderr, result.returncode
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
            stdout, stderr, code = e.stdout or b'', e.stderr or b'', None
            receipt['exception'] = 'collector timeout after 45 seconds'
        except OSError as e:
            stdout, stderr, code = b'', b'', None
            receipt['exception'] = repr(e)
        (dest / 'stdout.bin').write_bytes(stdout)
        (dest / 'stderr.bin').write_bytes(stderr)
        receipt.update({'end_utc': utc(), 'exit_code': code, 'stdout_bytes': len(stdout), 'stderr_bytes': len(stderr), 'stdout_sha256': sha(dest / 'stdout.bin'), 'stderr_sha256': sha(dest / 'stderr.bin')})
        success = code == 0
        try:
            body = json.loads(stdout)
            responses = body.get('tool_responses', [])
            success = success and bool(responses) and all(x.get('success') is not False for x in responses)
        except (ValueError, AttributeError, TypeError):
            success = False
        receipt['collector_success'] = success
        (dest / 'execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2) + '\n')
        errors = 0 if success else errors + 1
        err = (stderr + stdout[:4096] if not success else stderr).decode('utf-8', 'replace').lower()
        denied = any(x in err for x in ['rate limit', 'too many requests', 'permission denied', 'unauthorized', 'authentication failed', 'access denied'])
        print(json.dumps({'run_id': q['run_id'], 'schedule_position': q['schedule_position'], 'exit_code': code, 'success': success}), flush=True)
        if errors >= 3 or denied:
            (root / 'STOP.json').write_text(json.dumps({'at': q['run_id'], 'utc': utc(), 'consecutive_failures': errors, 'access_or_rate_error': denied}, indent=2) + '\n')
            break
        time.sleep(1)
    try:
        identity.update({'after_bytes': tool.stat().st_size, 'after_sha256': sha(tool)})
    except OSError as e:
        identity['after_error'] = repr(e)
    (root / 'tool-identity.json').write_text(json.dumps(identity, indent=2) + '\n')
    with (root / 'MANIFEST.csv').open('w', newline='') as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=['path', 'bytes', 'sha256'])
        w.writeheader()
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file() and p.name != 'MANIFEST.csv':
                w.writerow({'path': p.relative_to(root).as_posix(), 'bytes': p.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(p)})
    archive = root.with_suffix('.zip')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, 'x', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file():
                z.write(p, p.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({'archive_path': str(archive), 'bytes': archive.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(archive), 'attempted': len(list(root.glob('R*/execution.json'))), 'planned': 60, 'stopped': (root / 'STOP.json').exists()}), flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

import csv

import json
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import zipfile


def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():

    schedule = json.loads(schedule_path.read_text())
    assert len(schedule) == 12 and len({q['run_id'] for q in schedule}) == 12

        (dest / 'stderr.bin').write_bytes(stderr)
        receipt.update({'end_utc': utc(), 'exit_code': code, 'stdout_bytes': len(stdout), 'stderr_bytes': len(stderr), 'stdout_sha256': sha(dest / 'stdout.bin'), 'stderr_sha256': sha(dest / 'stderr.bin')})

            responses = body.get('tool_responses', [])
            success = success and bool(responses) and all(x.get('success') is not False for x in responses)
        except (ValueError, AttributeError, TypeError):
            success = False

        err = (stderr + stdout[:4096] if not success else stderr).decode('utf-8', 'replace').lower()
        denied = any(x in err for x in ['rate limit', 'too many requests', 'permission denied', 'unauthorized', 'authentication failed', 'access denied'])

        if errors >= 3 or denied:
            (root / 'STOP.json').write_text(json.dumps({'at': q['run_id'], 'utc': utc(), 'consecutive_failures': errors, 'access_or_rate_error': denied}, indent=2) + '\n')
            break
        time.sleep(1)
    try:
        identity.update({'after_bytes': tool.stat().st_size, 'after_sha256': sha(tool)})
    except OSError as e:
        identity['after_error'] = repr(e)
    (root / 'tool-identity.json').write_text(json.dumps(identity, indent=2) + '\n')
    with (root / 'MANIFEST.csv').open('w', newline='') as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=['path', 'bytes', 'sha256'])
        w.writeheader()
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file() and p.name != 'MANIFEST.csv':
                w.writerow({'path': p.relative_to(root).as_posix(), 'bytes': p.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(p)})

    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, 'x', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file():
                z.write(p, p.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

Collector (collector.py):
```python
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import zipfile


def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    schedule_path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    schedule = json.loads(schedule_path.read_text())
    assert len(schedule) == 30 and len({q['run_id'] for q in schedule}) == 30
    root = Path.cwd() / ('prenatal-audit-'  + datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ'))
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    (root / 'collector.py').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    (root / 'schedule.json').write_bytes(schedule_path.read_bytes())
    tool = Path('/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search')
    identity = {'path': str(tool)}
    try:
        identity.update({'before_bytes': tool.stat().st_size, 'before_sha256': sha(tool)})
    except OSError as e:
        identity['before_error'] = repr(e)
    errors = 0
    for q in schedule:
        dest = root / q['run_id']
        dest.mkdir()
        argv = [str(tool), '--query', q['query'], '-n', '50', '--conversation-id', 'prenatal-audit-20260927-Astra-fixed', '--retries', '0', '--timeout-secs', '30', '--raw', '--no-save']
        receipt = {'author': 'Astra (gpt-6-astra)', 'query': q, 'argv': argv, 'start_utc': utc(), 'exception': None}
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, timeout=45, check=False)
            stdout, stderr, code = result.stdout, result.stderr, result.returncode
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
            stdout, stderr, code = e.stdout or b'', e.stderr or b'', None
            receipt['exception'] = 'collector timeout after 45 seconds'
        except OSError as e:
            stdout, stderr, code = b'', b'', None
            receipt['exception'] = repr(e)
        (dest / 'stdout.bin').write_bytes(stdout)
        (dest / 'stderr.bin').write_bytes(stderr)
        receipt.update({'end_utc': utc(), 'exit_code': code, 'stdout_bytes': len(stdout), 'stderr_bytes': len(stderr), 'stdout_sha256': sha(dest / 'stdout.bin'), 'stderr_sha256': sha(dest / 'stderr.bin')})
        success = code == 0
        try:
            body = json.loads(stdout)
            responses = body.get('tool_responses', [])
            success = success and bool(responses) and all(x.get('success') is not False for x in responses)
        except (ValueError, AttributeError, TypeError):
            success = False
        receipt['collector_success'] = success
        (dest / 'execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2) + '\n')
        errors = 0 if success else errors + 1
        err = (stderr + stdout[:4096] if not success else stderr).decode('utf-8', 'replace').lower()
        denied = any(x in err for x in ['rate limit', 'too many requests', 'permission denied', 'unauthorized', 'authentication failed', 'access denied'])
        print(json.dumps({'run_id': q['run_id'], 'schedule_position': q['schedule_position'], 'exit_code': code, 'success': success}), flush=True)
        if errors >= 3 or denied:
            (root / 'STOP.json').write_text(json.dumps({'at': q['run_id'], 'utc': utc(), 'consecutive_failures': errors, 'access_or_rate_error': denied}, indent=2) + '\n')
            break
        time.sleep(1)
    try:
        identity.update({'after_bytes': tool.stat().st_size, 'after_sha256': sha(tool)})
    except OSError as e:
        identity['after_error'] = repr(e)
    (root / 'tool-identity.json').write_text(json.dumps(identity, indent=2) + '\n')
    with (root / 'MANIFEST.csv').open('w', newline='') as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=['path', 'bytes', 'sha256'])
        w.writeheader()
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file() and p.name != 'MANIFEST.csv':
                w.writerow({'path': p.relative_to(root).as_posix(), 'bytes': p.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(p)})
    archive = root.with_suffix('.zip')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, 'x', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file():
                z.write(p, p.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({'archive_path': str(archive), 'bytes': archive.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(archive), 'attempted': len(list(root.glob('R*/execution.json'))), 'planned': 30, 'stopped': (root / 'STOP.json').exists()}), flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

```python
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import zipfile


def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    schedule_path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    schedule = json.loads(schedule_path.read_text())
    assert len(schedule) == 9 and len({q['run_id'] for q in schedule}) == 9
    root = Path.cwd() / ('prenatal-brand-audit-'  + datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ'))
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    (root / 'collector.py').write_bytes(Path(__file__).read_bytes())
    (root / 'schedule.json').write_bytes(schedule_path.read_bytes())
    tool = Path('/opt/hatch/bin/meta-catalog-search')
    identity = {'path': str(tool)}
    try:
        identity.update({'before_bytes': tool.stat().st_size, 'before_sha256': sha(tool)})
    except OSError as e:
        identity['before_error'] = repr(e)
    errors = 0
    for q in schedule:
        dest = root / q['run_id']
        dest.mkdir()
        argv = [str(tool), '--query', q['query'], '-n', '50', '--conversation-id', 'prenatal-audit-20260927-Astra-fixed', '--retries', '0', '--timeout-secs', '30', '--raw', '--no-save']
        receipt = {'author': 'Astra (gpt-6-astra)', 'query': q, 'argv': argv, 'start_utc': utc(), 'exception': None}
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, timeout=45, check=False)
            stdout, stderr, code = result.stdout, result.stderr, result.returncode
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
            stdout, stderr, code = e.stdout or b'', e.stderr or b'', None
            receipt['exception'] = 'collector timeout after 45 seconds'
        except OSError as e:
            stdout, stderr, code = b'', b'', None
            receipt['exception'] = repr(e)
        (dest / 'stdout.bin').write_bytes(stdout)
        (dest / 'stderr.bin').write_bytes(stderr)
        receipt.update({'end_utc': utc(), 'exit_code': code, 'stdout_bytes': len(stdout), 'stderr_bytes': len(stderr), 'stdout_sha256': sha(dest / 'stdout.bin'), 'stderr_sha256': sha(dest / 'stderr.bin')})
        success = code == 0
        try:
            body = json.loads(stdout)
            responses = body.get('tool_responses', [])
            success = success and bool(responses) and all(x.get('success') is not False for x in responses)
        except (ValueError, AttributeError, TypeError):
            success = False
        receipt['collector_success'] = success
        (dest / 'execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt, indent=2) + '\n')
        errors = 0 if success else errors + 1
        err = (stderr + stdout[:4096] if not success else stderr).decode('utf-8', 'replace').lower()
        denied = any(x in err for x in ['rate limit', 'too many requests', 'permission denied', 'unauthorized', 'authentication failed', 'access denied'])
        print(json.dumps({'run_id': q['run_id'], 'schedule_position': q['schedule_position'], 'exit_code': code, 'success': success}), flush=True)
        if errors >= 3 or denied:
            (root / 'STOP.json').write_text(json.dumps({'at': q['run_id'], 'utc': utc(), 'consecutive_failures': errors, 'access_or_rate_error': denied}, indent=2) + '\n')
            break
        time.sleep(1)
    try:
        identity.update({'after_bytes': tool.stat().st_size, 'after_sha256': sha(tool)})
    except OSError as e:
        identity['after_error'] = repr(e)
    (root / 'tool-identity.json').write_text(json.dumps(identity, indent=2) + '\n')
    with (root / 'MANIFEST.csv').open('w', newline='') as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=['path', 'bytes', 'sha256'])
        w.writeheader()
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file() and p.name != 'MANIFEST.csv':
                w.writerow({'path': p.relative_to(root).as_posix(), 'bytes': p.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(p)})
    archive = root.with_suffix('.zip')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, 'x', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
        for p in sorted(root.rglob('*')):
            if p.is_file():
                z.write(p, p.relative_to(root.parent).as_posix())
    print(json.dumps({'archive_path': str(archive), 'bytes': archive.stat().st_size, 'sha256': sha(archive), 'attempted': len(list(root.glob('R*/execution.json'))), 'planned': 9, 'stopped': (root / 'STOP.json').exists()}), flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```
